- Request: `client_version: int`, `device_type: int`, `dummy: string`
- Response: `<body>k__BackingField: VersionCheckResultProtocol.BODY`
  - `VersionCheckResultProtocol.BODY`: `<redirect_sgf_server>k__BackingField: string`, `photon_server_infos: List<SocketFrame.PHOTON_SERVER_INFO>`, `<country_code>k__BackingField: string`, `server_root_info: bsServerRootData`
  - `SocketFrame.PHOTON_SERVER_INFO`: `<room_region>k__BackingField: int`, `<region>k__BackingField: int`, `<redirect_address>k__BackingField: string`

## weapon_lock.php

`bsNetReqWeaponLock` -> `WeaponLockResultProtocol`

- Request: `protector_array: List<bsNetReqWeaponLock.LockItem>`, `sub_weapon_array: List<bsNetReqWeaponLock.LockItem>`, `weapon_array: List<bsNetReqWeaponLock.LockItem>`, `dummy: string`
- Response: `<body>k__BackingField: WeaponLockResultProtocol.BODY`
  - `bsNetReqWeaponLock.LockItem`: `id: long`, `lock_flag: bool`
  - `WeaponLockResultProtocol.BODY`: `Weapon: List<UserDataTbResultProtocol.USER_DATA_RECORD_weapon>`, `SubWeapon: List<UserDataTbResultProtocol.USER_DATA_RECORD_subweapon>`, `Protector: List<UserDataTbResultProtocol.USER_DATA_RECORD_protector>`
  - `UserDataTbResultProtocol.USER_DATA_RECORD_weapon`: `<id>k__BackingField: long`, `<mst_weapon_id>k__BackingField: int`, `<level>k__BackingField: int`, `<max_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<exp>k__BackingField: int`, `<skill_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<skill_exp>k__BackingField: int`, `<boost_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<limitbreak_count>k__BackingField: int`, `<lock_flag>k__BackingField: int`, `<insert_date>k__BackingField: string`, `isNew: bool`, `<limitbreak2nd_count>k__BackingField: int`
  - `UserDataTbResultProtocol.USER_DATA_RECORD_subweapon`: `<id>k__BackingField: long`, `<mst_sub_weapon_id>k__BackingField: int`, `<level>k__BackingField: int`, `<max_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<exp>k__BackingField: int`, `<skill_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<skill_exp>k__BackingField: int`, `<boost_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<limitbreak_count>k__BackingField: int`, `<lock_flag>k__BackingField: int`, `<insert_date>k__BackingField: string`, `isNew: bool`, `<limitbreak2nd_count>k__BackingField: int`
  - `UserDataTbResultProtocol.USER_DATA_RECORD_protector`: `<id>k__BackingField: long`, `<mst_protector_id>k__BackingField: int`, `<level>k__BackingField: int`, `<max_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<exp>k__BackingField: int`, `<boost_level>k__BackingField: int`, `<limitbreak_count>k__BackingField: int`, `<lock_flag>k__BackingField: int`, `<insert_date>k__BackingField: string`, `isNew: bool`, `<limitbreak2nd_count>k__BackingField: int`

## world_chat_busy_info.php

`bsNetReqWorldChatBusyInfo` -> `WorldChatBusyInfoResultProtocol`

- Request: `start_channel_id: long`, `end_channel_id: long`, `btm: bool`
- Response: `<body>k__BackingField: WorldChatBusyInfoResultProtocol.BODY`
  - `WorldChatBusyInfoResultProtocol.BODY`: `busy_infos: List<BUSY_INFO>`
